<p><a href="https://pypi.org/project/langgraph/"><img src="https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/langgraph.svg" alt="Version"></a>
<a href="https://pepy.tech/project/langgraph"><img src="https://static.pepy.tech/badge/langgraph/month" alt="Downloads"></a>
<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph/issues"><img src="https://img.shields.io/github/issues-raw/langchain-ai/langgraph" alt="Open Issues"></a>
<a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/overview"><img src="https://img.shields.io/badge/docs-latest-blue" alt="Docs"></a></p>
<p>Trusted by companies shaping the future of agents – including Klarna, Replit, Elastic, and more – LangGraph is a low-level orchestration framework for building, managing, and deploying long-running, stateful agents.</p>
<h2 id="get-started">Get started</h2>
<p>Install LangGraph:</p>
<pre><code>pip install -U langgraph
</code></pre>
<p>Create a simple workflow:</p>
<pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8" tabindex="0"><code><span class="line"><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> langgraph.graph </span><span style="color:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="color:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> START</span><span style="color:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, StateGraph</span></span>

<p>Get started with the <a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/quickstart">LangGraph Quickstart</a>.</p>
<p>To quickly build agents with LangChain's <code>create_agent</code> (built on LangGraph), see the <a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/agents">LangChain Agents documentation</a>.</p>
<h2 id="core-benefits">Core benefits</h2>
<p>LangGraph provides low-level supporting infrastructure for <em>any</em> long-running, stateful workflow or agent. LangGraph does not abstract prompts or architecture, and provides the following central benefits:</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/durable-execution">Durable execution</a>: Build agents that persist through failures and can run for extended periods, automatically resuming from exactly where they left off.</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/interrupts">Human-in-the-loop</a>: Seamlessly incorporate human oversight by inspecting and modifying agent state at any point during execution.</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/memory">Comprehensive memory</a>: Create truly stateful agents with both short-term working memory for ongoing reasoning and long-term persistent memory across sessions.</li>
<li><a href="http://www.langchain.com/langsmith">Debugging with LangSmith</a>: Gain deep visibility into complex agent behavior with visualization tools that trace execution paths, capture state transitions, and provide detailed runtime metrics.</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.langchain.com/langsmith/app-development">Production-ready deployment</a>: Deploy sophisticated agent systems confidently with scalable infrastructure designed to handle the unique challenges of stateful, long-running workflows.</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="langgraphs-ecosystem">LangGraph’s ecosystem</h2>
<p>While LangGraph can be used standalone, it also integrates seamlessly with any LangChain product, giving developers a full suite of tools for building agents. To improve your LLM application development, pair LangGraph with:</p>
<ul>
<li><a href="http://www.langchain.com/langsmith">LangSmith</a> — Helpful for agent evals and observability. Debug poor-performing LLM app runs, evaluate agent trajectories, gain visibility in production, and improve performance over time.</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.langchain.com/langsmith/deployments">LangSmith Deployment</a> — Deploy and scale agents effortlessly with a purpose-built deployment platform for long running, stateful workflows. Discover, reuse, configure, and share agents across teams — and iterate quickly with visual prototyping in <a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/studio">LangGraph Studio</a>.</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/overview">LangChain</a> – Provides integrations and composable components to streamline LLM application development.</li>
</ul>
<blockquote>
<p>[!NOTE]
Looking for the JS version of LangGraph? See the <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraphjs">JS repo</a> and the <a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/javascript/langgraph/overview">JS docs</a>.</p>
</blockquote>
<h2 id="additional-resources">Additional resources</h2>
<ul>
<li><a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/guides">Guides</a>: Quick, actionable code snippets for topics such as streaming, adding memory &#x26; persistence, and design patterns (e.g. branching, subgraphs, etc.).</li>
<li><a href="https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/">Reference</a>: Detailed reference on core classes, methods, how to use the graph and checkpointing APIs, and higher-level prebuilt components.</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/agentic-rag">Examples</a>: Guided examples on getting started with LangGraph.</li>
<li><a href="https://forum.langchain.com/">LangChain Forum</a>: Connect with the community and share all of your technical questions, ideas, and feedback.</li>
<li><a href="https://academy.langchain.com/courses/intro-to-langgraph">LangChain Academy</a>: Learn the basics of LangGraph in our free, structured course.</li>
<li><a href="https://www.langchain.com/built-with-langgraph">Case studies</a>: Hear how industry leaders use LangGraph to ship AI applications at scale.</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="acknowledgements">Acknowledgements</h2>
<p>LangGraph is inspired by <a href="https://research.google/pubs/pub37252/">Pregel</a> and <a href="https://beam.apache.org/">Apache Beam</a>. The public interface draws inspiration from <a href="https://networkx.org/documentation/latest/">NetworkX</a>. langgraph\n\n\u003e Python package\n\n📖 [View in docs](https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph)\n\n\u003cpicture class=\"github-only\"\u003e\n  \u003csource media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: light)\" srcset=\"https://langchain-ai.github.io/langgraph/static/wordmark_dark.svg\"\u003e\n  \u003csource media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\" srcset=\"https://langchain-ai.github.io/langgraph/static/wordmark_light.svg\"\u003e\n  \u003cimg alt=\"LangGraph Logo\" src=\"https://langchain-ai.github.io/langgraph/static/wordmark_dark.svg\" width=\"80%\"\u003e\n\u003c/picture\u003e\n\n\u003cdiv\u003e\n\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\n[![Version](https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/langgraph.svg)](https://pypi.org/project/langgraph/)\n[![Downloads](https://static.pepy.tech/badge/langgraph/month)](https://pepy.tech/project/langgraph)\n[![Open Issues](https://img.shields.io/github/issues-raw/langchain-ai/langgraph)](https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph/issues)\n[![Docs](https://img.shields.io/badge/docs-latest-blue)](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/overview)\n\nTrusted by companies shaping the future of agents – including Klarna, Replit, Elastic, and more – LangGraph is a low-level orchestration framework for building, managing, and deploying long-running, stateful agents.\n\n## Get started\n\nInstall LangGraph:\n\n```\npip install -U langgraph\n```\n\nCreate a simple workflow:\n\n```python\nfrom langgraph.graph import START, StateGraph\nfrom typing_extensions import TypedDict\n\n\nclass State(TypedDict):\n    text: str\n\n\ndef node_a(state: State) -\u003e dict:\n    return {\"text\": state[\"text\"] + \"a\"}\n\n\ndef node_b(state: State) -\u003e dict:\n    return {\"text\": state[\"text\"] + \"b\"}\n\n\ngraph = StateGraph(State)\ngraph.add_node(\"node_a\", node_a)\ngraph.add_node(\"node_b\", node_b)\ngraph.add_edge(START, \"node_a\")\ngraph.add_edge(\"node_a\", \"node_b\")\n\nprint(graph.compile().invoke({\"text\": \"\"}))\n# {'text': 'ab'}\n```\n\nGet started with the [LangGraph Quickstart](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/quickstart).\n\nTo quickly build agents with LangChain's `create_agent` (built on LangGraph), see the [LangChain Agents documentation](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/agents).\n\n## Core benefits\n\nLangGraph provides low-level supporting infrastructure for *any* long-running, stateful workflow or agent. LangGraph does not abstract prompts or architecture, and provides the following central benefits:\n\n- [Durable execution](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/durable-execution): Build agents that persist through failures and can run for extended periods, automatically resuming from exactly where they left off.\n- [Human-in-the-loop](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/interrupts): Seamlessly incorporate human oversight by inspecting and modifying agent state at any point during execution.\n- [Comprehensive memory](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/memory): Create truly stateful agents with both short-term working memory for ongoing reasoning and long-term persistent memory across sessions.\n- [Debugging with LangSmith](http://www.langchain.com/langsmith): Gain deep visibility into complex agent behavior with visualization tools that trace execution paths, capture state transitions, and provide detailed runtime metrics.\n- [Production-ready deployment](https://docs.langchain.com/langsmith/app-development): Deploy sophisticated agent systems confidently with scalable infrastructure designed to handle the unique challenges of stateful, long-running workflows.\n\n## LangGraph’s ecosystem\n\nWhile LangGraph can be used standalone, it also integrates seamlessly with any LangChain product, giving developers a full suite of tools for building agents. To improve your LLM application development, pair LangGraph with:\n\n- [LangSmith](http://www.langchain.com/langsmith) — Helpful for agent evals and observability. Debug poor-performing LLM app runs, evaluate agent trajectories, gain visibility in production, and improve performance over time.\n- [LangSmith Deployment](https://docs.langchain.com/langsmith/deployments) — Deploy and scale agents effortlessly with a purpose-built deployment platform for long running, stateful workflows. Discover, reuse, configure, and share agents across teams — and iterate quickly with visual prototyping in [LangGraph Studio](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/studio).\n- [LangChain](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/overview) – Provides integrations and composable components to streamline LLM application development.\n\n\u003e [!NOTE]\n\u003e Looking for the JS version of LangGraph? See the [JS repo](https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraphjs) and the [JS docs](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/javascript/langgraph/overview).\n\n## Additional resources\n\n- [Guides](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/guides): Quick, actionable code snippets for topics such as streaming, adding memory \u0026 persistence, and design patterns (e.g. branching, subgraphs, etc.).\n- [Reference](https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/): Detailed reference on core classes, methods, how to use the graph and checkpointing APIs, and higher-level prebuilt components.\n- [Examples](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/agentic-rag): Guided examples on getting started with LangGraph.\n- [LangChain Forum](https://forum.langchain.com/): Connect with the community and share all of your technical questions, ideas, and feedback.\n- [LangChain Academy](https://academy.langchain.com/courses/intro-to-langgraph): Learn the basics of LangGraph in our free, structured course.\n- [Case studies](https://www.langchain.com/built-with-langgraph): Hear how industry leaders use LangGraph to ship AI applications at scale.\n\n## Acknowledgements\n\nLangGraph is inspired by [Pregel](https://research.google/pubs/pub37252/) and [Apache Beam](https://beam.apache.org/). The public interface draws inspiration from [NetworkX](https://networkx.org/documentation/latest/). LangGraph is built by LangChain Inc, the creators of LangChain, but can be used without LangChain.\n\n## Classes\n\n- [`ErrorCode`](https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/errors/ErrorCode)\n- [`GraphRecursionError`](https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/errors/GraphRecursionError)\n- LangGraph does not abstract prompts or architecture, and provides the following central benefits:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/durable-execution\"\u003eDurable execution\u003c/a\u003e: Build agents that persist through failures and can run for extended periods, automatically resuming from exactly where they left off.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/interrupts\"\u003eHuman-in-the-loop\u003c/a\u003e: Seamlessly incorporate human oversight by inspecting and modifying agent state at any point during execution.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/memory\"\u003eComprehensive memory\u003c/a\u003e: Create truly stateful agents with both short-term working memory for ongoing reasoning and long-term persistent memory across sessions.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"http://www.langchain.com/langsmith\"\u003eDebugging with LangSmith\u003c/a\u003e: Gain deep visibility into complex agent behavior with visualization tools that trace execution paths, capture state transitions, and provide detailed runtime metrics.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/langsmith/app-development\"\u003eProduction-ready deployment\u003c/a\u003e: Deploy sophisticated agent systems confidently with scalable infrastructure designed to handle the unique challenges of stateful, long-running workflows.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003ch2 id=\"langgraphs-ecosystem\"\u003eLangGraph’s ecosystem\u003c/h2\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eWhile LangGraph can be used standalone, it also integrates seamlessly with any LangChain product, giving developers a full suite of tools for building agents. To improve your LLM application development, pair LangGraph with:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"http://www.langchain.com/langsmith\"\u003eLangSmith\u003c/a\u003e — Helpful for agent evals and observability. Debug poor-performing LLM app runs, evaluate agent trajectories, gain visibility in production, and improve performance over time.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/langsmith/deployments\"\u003eLangSmith Deployment\u003c/a\u003e — Deploy and scale agents effortlessly with a purpose-built deployment platform for long running, stateful workflows. Discover, reuse, configure, and share agents across teams — and iterate quickly with visual prototyping in \u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/studio\"\u003eLangGraph Studio\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/overview\"\u003eLangChain\u003c/a\u003e – Provides integrations and composable components to streamline LLM application development.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cblockquote\u003e\n\u003cp\u003e[!NOTE]\nLooking for the JS version of LangGraph? The public interface draws inspiration from \u003ca href=\"https://networkx.org/documentation/latest/\"\u003eNetworkX\u003c/a\u003e. media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: light)\" srcset=\"https://langchain-ai.github.io/langgraph/static/wordmark_dark.svg\"\u003e\n  \u003csource media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\" srcset=\"https://langchain-ai.github.io/langgraph/static/wordmark_light.svg\"\u003e\n  \u003cimg alt=\"LangGraph Logo\" src=\"https://langchain-ai.github.io/langgraph/static/wordmark_dark.svg\" width=\"80%\"\u003e\n\u003c/picture\u003e\n\n\u003cdiv\u003e\n\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\n[![Version](https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/langgraph.svg)](https://pypi.org/project/langgraph/)\n[![Downloads](https://static.pepy.tech/badge/langgraph/month)](https://pepy.tech/project/langgraph)\n[![Open Issues](https://img.shields.io/github/issues-raw/langchain-ai/langgraph)](https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraph/issues)\n[![Docs](https://img.shields.io/badge/docs-latest-blue)](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/overview)\n\nTrusted by companies shaping the future of agents – including Klarna, Replit, Elastic, and more – LangGraph is a low-level orchestration framework for building, managing, and deploying long-running, stateful agents.\n\n## Get started\n\nInstall LangGraph:\n\n```\npip install -U langgraph\n```\n\nCreate a simple workflow:\n\n```python\nfrom langgraph.graph import START, StateGraph\nfrom typing_extensions import TypedDict\n\n\nclass State(TypedDict):\n    text: str\n\n\ndef node_a(state: State) -\u003e dict:\n    return {\"text\": state[\"text\"] + \"a\"}\n\n\ndef node_b(state: State) -\u003e dict:\n    return {\"text\": state[\"text\"] + \"b\"}\n\n\ngraph = StateGraph(State)\ngraph.add_node(\"node_a\", node_a)\ngraph.add_node(\"node_b\", node_b)\ngraph.add_edge(START, \"node_a\")\ngraph.add_edge(\"node_a\", \"node_b\")\n\nprint(graph.compile().invoke({\"text\": \"\"}))\n# {'text': 'ab'}\n```\n\nGet started with the [LangGraph Quickstart](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/quickstart).\n\nTo quickly build agents with LangChain's `create_agent` (built on LangGraph), see the [LangChain Agents documentation](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/agents).\n\n## Core benefits\n\nLangGraph provides low-level supporting infrastructure for *any* long-running, stateful workflow or agent. LangGraph does not abstract prompts or architecture, and provides the following central benefits:\n\n- [Durable execution](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/durable-execution): Build agents that persist through failures and can run for extended periods, automatically resuming from exactly where they left off.\n- [Human-in-the-loop](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/interrupts): Seamlessly incorporate human oversight by inspecting and modifying agent state at any point during execution.\n- [Comprehensive memory](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/memory): Create truly stateful agents with both short-term working memory for ongoing reasoning and long-term persistent memory across sessions.\n- [Debugging with LangSmith](http://www.langchain.com/langsmith): Gain deep visibility into complex agent behavior with visualization tools that trace execution paths, capture state transitions, and provide detailed runtime metrics.\n- [Production-ready deployment](https://docs.langchain.com/langsmith/app-development): Deploy sophisticated agent systems confidently with scalable infrastructure designed to handle the unique challenges of stateful, long-running workflows.\n\n## LangGraph’s ecosystem\n\nWhile LangGraph can be used standalone, it also integrates seamlessly with any LangChain product, giving developers a full suite of tools for building agents. To improve your LLM application development, pair LangGraph with:\n\n- [LangSmith](http://www.langchain.com/langsmith) — Helpful for agent evals and observability. Debug poor-performing LLM app runs, evaluate agent trajectories, gain visibility in production, and improve performance over time.\n- [LangSmith Deployment](https://docs.langchain.com/langsmith/deployments) — Deploy and scale agents effortlessly with a purpose-built deployment platform for long running, stateful workflows. Discover, reuse, configure, and share agents across teams — and iterate quickly with visual prototyping in [LangGraph Studio](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/studio).\n- [LangChain](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langchain/overview) – Provides integrations and composable components to streamline LLM application development.\n\n\u003e [!NOTE]\n\u003e Looking for the JS version of LangGraph? See the [JS repo](https://github.com/langchain-ai/langgraphjs) and the [JS docs](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/javascript/langgraph/overview).\n\n## Additional resources\n\n- [Guides](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/guides): Quick, actionable code snippets for topics such as streaming, adding memory \u0026 persistence, and design patterns (e.g. branching, subgraphs, etc.).\n- [Reference](https://reference.langchain.com/python/langgraph/): Detailed reference on core classes, methods, how to use the graph and checkpointing APIs, and higher-level prebuilt components.\n- [Examples](https://docs.langchain.com/oss/python/langgraph/agentic-rag): Guided examples on getting started with LangGraph.\n- [LangChain Forum](https://forum.langchain.com/): Connect with the community and share all of your technical questions, ideas, and feedback.\n- [LangChain Academy](https://academy.langchain.com/courses/intro-to-langgraph): Learn the basics of LangGraph in our free, structured course.\n- [Case studies](https://www.langchain.com/built-with-langgraph): Hear how industry leaders use LangGraph to ship AI applications at scale.\n\n## Acknowledgements\n\nLangGraph is inspired by [Pregel](https://research.google/pubs/pub37252/) and [Apache Beam](https://beam.apache.org/). The public interface draws inspiration from [NetworkX](https://networkx.org/documentation/latest/). LangGraph is built by LangChain Inc, the creators of LangChain, but can be used without LangChain."])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"2d:[\"$\",\"$L11c\",null,{\"html\":\"$11d\",\"rawContent\":\"$11e\",\"className\":\"\",\"compact\":false}]\n1e:[[\"$\",\"meta\",\"0\",{\"charSet\":\"utf-8\"}],[\"$\",\"meta\",\"1\",{\"name\":\"viewport\",\"content\":\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1\"}]]\n1c:null\n"])</script><script>self.__next_f.push([1,"20:[[\"$\",\"title\",\"0\",{\"children\":\"langgraph | LangChain Reference\"}],[\"$\",\"meta\",\"1\",{\"name\":\"description\",\"content\":\"Python API reference for langgraph.
